from nltk.sentiment import SentimentIntensityAnalyzer
import spacy

**Amazon Review Summary Generator**
=====================================

### Task Description

Generate a summary of Amazon product reviews.

### Input Format

* Review text in plain text format

### Output Format

* Summary of the review in a concise and informative format

### Code
```python
import re

def generate_summary(review_text):
    """
    Generate a summary of the review
-----
-----
